Qwen MHA2SHA attention subgraph test record

Purpose:
Compare qwen3-like and qwen3.5-like full-attention subgraphs without exporting the
full decoder. Each script run appends a JSON result below.

Baseline graph differences:
qwen3-0.6B-like:
- dim=1024, n_heads=16, n_kv_heads=8, head_dim=128
- full RoPE, QK norm before RoPE, no q-gate
- GQA repeat count 2

qwen3.5-0.8B-like:
- dim=1024, n_heads=8, n_kv_heads=2, head_dim=256
- partial RoPE 0.25, QK norm before RoPE, q-gate
- GQA repeat count 4

Local findings before this script:
- Running ConvertMhaToSha on the raw Edge graph is not equivalent to the QNN
  capture/preprocess path; the raw graph can present a 3D bmm-style attention
  shape and hit cat(axis=3).
- After using the default QNN capture passes, ConvertMhaToSha preserves outputs
  for qwen3-like and qwen3.5-like LlamaAttention subgraphs at ar_len=1 and ar_len=4.
- Fixed a ConvertMhaToSha metadata copy bug where src_tensor_meta was assigned
  a boolean because of walrus/operator precedence.
